既存トップのリンク分類やL7未着手ラベルも受け入れ結果に合わせ最小差分で調整。ルート・L7・公開導線は Claude の担当。本作から既存トップへの戻りリンクは実装済み。

## 今足さないもの

反応拡散・粘菌・L-system、3D、AI画像、音・BPM、動画、SVG、JPG、履歴タイムライン、ギャラリーは別発注。次にモデルを追加する場合も snapshot の由来と同条件比較を先に設計する。未実装機能の空ボタンは置かない。

## v0.2 受け入れ時の確認

1. `index.html`をダブルクリックし、③→TAKE A→3構図→PNG／JSON保存→JSON読込を実操作で確認する。Chrome headlessの自動検証結果は03とtest-results.jsonを参照。
2. ZOOM=1/1.8/3で粒子のタイル、重心追跡、FREEZEの連続性を確認。実機の手触り、Safari/iOS、GPU負荷は未検証。
3. `verification/claude-review/*.cjs` のES module動的importは、Claude側で `window.EC.composition` / `window.EC.render` 等へ移す必要がある。保護されたレビューコードはこちらでは変更していない。既存`__EC`メソッドと指定DOM IDの改名なし。viewとsetZoomのみ追加。
4. 提出物はビルド不要のclassic script。npmやCDNを導入しない。画像・masterplan・既存トップ・.lnk・_Archiveは触らない。
/* Existing test-runtime only; the artwork has no dependencies or build step.
   Run: node expression-composer/verification/verify-browser.cjs
   Optional EC_PLAYWRIGHT / EC_CHROME point to already installed tools. */
const fs = require('node:fs'), path = require('node:path'), http = require('node:http');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');
const out = __dirname, root = path.resolve(out, '../..');
const temp = path.join(out, 'browser-temp');
fs.mkdirSync(temp, { recursive: true });
process.env.TEMP = temp; process.env.TMP = temp;
const { chromium } = require(process.env.EC_PLAYWRIGHT || 'C:/Users/so173/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/playwright');
const shots = path.join(out, 'screenshots'); fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true });
const report = { date: new Date().toISOString(), backend: 'Headless Chrome / --disable-gpu / synthetic input / DPR 1', checks: [], metrics: [], errors: [], screenshots: [] };
const check = (name, pass, evidence) => { report.checks.push({ name, result: pass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL', evidence }); if (!pass) console.log('FAIL', name, JSON.stringify(evidence)); };
const hash = text => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex');
const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  try {
    let file = path.resolve(root, '.' + decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost').pathname));
    if (!file.startsWith(root + path.sep)) { res.writeHead(403).end(); return; }
    if (fs.statSync(file).isDirectory()) file = path.join(file, 'index.html');
    res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': ({ '.js': 'text/javascript', '.html': 'text/html', '.css': 'text/css', '.json': 'application/json' })[path.extname(file)] || 'application/octet-stream' });
    fs.createReadStream(file).pipe(res);
  } catch { res.writeHead(404).end(); }
});
async function run() {
  await new Promise(resolve => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  let browser;
  try {
    browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, executablePath: process.env.EC_CHROME || 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', args: ['--disable-gpu'] });
    report.browser = browser.version();
    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 }, deviceScaleFactor: 1, acceptDownloads: true });
    page.on('pageerror', e => report.errors.push(String(e)));
    page.on('console', m => { if (m.type() === 'error') report.errors.push(m.text()); });
    page.on('response', r => { if (r.status() >= 400) report.errors.push(`${r.status()} ${r.url()}`); });
    page.on('request', r => { if (!r.url().startsWith(base) && !r.url().startsWith('data:')) report.errors.push('External request: ' + r.url()); });
    await page.goto(base + '/expression-composer/', { waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__EC);
    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); });
    async function shot(name) {
      await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, name + '.png'), fullPage: true }); report.screenshots.push(name + '.png');
    }
    await shot('1440x900-seed');
    const deterministic = await page.evaluate(() => [__EC.simDeterminism(42, 600), __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600)]);
    check('simDeterminism(42,600) exact equality', deterministic[0] === deterministic[1], deterministic);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'BEGIN', exact: false }).click();
    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); });
    const ruleBefore = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.getState().rules.align);
    await page.locator('#rule-align').focus(); await page.keyboard.press('ArrowRight');
    check('RULE keyboard slider updates coefficient', await page.evaluate(() => __EC.getState().rules.align) > ruleBefore);
    await page.evaluate(() => __EC.setRules({ align: 1.5 }));
    const ruleChange = await page.evaluate(() => {
      __EC.reset(); __EC.step(100); const a = __EC.take('main');
      __EC.setRules({ alignOn: false }); __EC.reset(); __EC.step(100); const b = __EC.take('main');
      return { a, b };
    });
    check('Rule changes alter real particle state', ruleChange.a !== ruleChange.b, ruleChange);
    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.setRules({ separate: 1.4, align: 1.5, cohere: 0.65, noise: 0.03, alignOn: true }); __EC.compare(true); __EC.pause(true); });
    const initial = await page.evaluate(() => { const a = __EC.take('A'), b = __EC.take('B'); __EC.compare(true); return { a, b }; });
    check('A/B initial particle state identical', initial.a === initial.b, initial);
    const stats = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.step(600); return __EC.compareStats(); });
    check('A/B same step', stats.stepA === stats.stepB && stats.stepA === 600, stats);
    report.metrics.push({ name: 'Phi seed42 default step600', ...stats, onHigher: stats.phiA > stats.phiB });
    await shot('compare-step600');
    const selectedB = await page.evaluate(() => { const h = __EC.take('B'); return { h, snapshot: __EC.exportJSON().snapshot }; });
    check('TAKE B records actual side / rules', selectedB.snapshot.side === 'B' && !selectedB.snapshot.rules.alignOn && selectedB.snapshot.step === 600, { hash: selectedB.h });
    // Recreate the same comparison and sample the particle-preserving transition.
    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); });
    await shot('freeze-before-A');
    await page.locator('#take-a').click();
    const transition = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.diagnostics());
    check('FREEZE retains all 600 source IDs', transition.transition?.ids.length === 600 && transition.transition.ids.every((id, i) => id === i) && transition.frozen, transition.transition && { count: transition.transition.ids.length, duration: transition.transition.duration });
    await shot('freeze-start'); await page.waitForTimeout(350); await shot('freeze-mid');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => __EC.getState().phase === 'EDIT'); await shot('freeze-after');
    const immutable = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const before = __EC.snapshotHash(), meta = __EC.getState().snapshot;
      __EC.setRules({ align: 0, cohere: 2 }); __EC.step(3);
      return { before, after: __EC.snapshotHash(), meta, afterMeta: __EC.getState().snapshot, frozen: __EC.diagnostics().frozen };
    });
    check('Snapshot immutable after rule and simulation changes', immutable.before === immutable.after && JSON.stringify(immutable.meta) === JSON.stringify(immutable.afterMeta) && immutable.frozen, immutable);
    const posterData = {};
const fs = require('node:fs'), path = require('node:path'), cp = require('node:child_process'), crypto = require('node:crypto');
const app = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
const files = ['index.html', 'styles.css', 'README.md'];
function walk(dir) { if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(app, dir))) return; for (const e of fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, dir), { withFileTypes: true })) { const rel = `${dir}/${e.name}`; if (e.isDirectory()) { if (e.name !== 'screenshots' && e.name !== 'browser-temp') walk(rel); } else if (e.name !== 'static-results.json' && /\.(js|cjs|mjs|md|html|css|json)$/.test(e.name)) files.push(rel); } }
for (const dir of ['js', 'docs', 'verification']) walk(dir);
if (fs.existsSync(path.join(app, '_codex/REPORT_01.md'))) files.push('_codex/REPORT_01.md');
const report = { date: new Date().toISOString(), files: [], errors: [] };
for (const relative of [...new Set(files)]) {
  const file = path.join(app, relative);
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) { report.errors.push('Missing ' + relative); continue; }
  const bytes = fs.readFileSync(file);
  let utf8 = true; try { new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes); } catch { utf8 = false; }
  const row = { file: relative, utf8, cr: bytes.includes(13), bom: bytes[0] === 239 && bytes[1] === 187 && bytes[2] === 191,
    sha256: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex') };
  if (!utf8 || row.cr || row.bom) report.errors.push('Encoding ' + relative);
  if (/\.(js|cjs|mjs)$/.test(relative)) { const r = cp.spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--check', file], { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true }); row.syntax = r.status === 0; if (!row.syntax) report.errors.push(relative + ': ' + r.stderr); }
  report.files.push(row);
}
const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
for (const m of html.matchAll(/(?:src|href)="([^"]+)"/g)) {
  if (/^(data:|https?:|#)/.test(m[1])) continue;
  if (!fs.existsSync(path.resolve(app, m[1]))) report.errors.push('Missing link: ' + m[1]);
}
report.summary = { files: report.files.length, syntax: report.files.filter(x => x.syntax).length, errors: report.errors.length };
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'static-results.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
console.log(JSON.stringify(report.summary)); if (report.errors.length) { console.log(report.errors); process.exitCode = 1; }
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import vm from 'node:vm';
globalThis.window = { EC: {} };
for (const name of ['sim', 'compare', 'composition', 'snapshot']) vm.runInThisContext(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../js/' + name + '.js', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
const { createSim, stepSim, hashParticles } = window.EC.sim;
const { createComparison, stepComparison, comparisonStats } = window.EC.compare;
const { takeSnapshot, validateDocument } = window.EC.snapshot;
const { DEFAULT_EDIT } = window.EC.composition;
const report = { node: process.version, date: new Date().toISOString(), checks: [] };
const simulations = [stepSim(createSim(42), 600), stepSim(createSim(42), 600)];
const hashes = simulations.map(s => hashParticles(s.particles));
report.checks.push({ name: 'Same engine fresh simulation equality', pass: hashes[0] === hashes[1], hashes });
const pair = createComparison(42, undefined, 600); stepComparison(pair, 600);
report.comparison = comparisonStats(pair);
report.checks.push({ name: 'A/B synchronous steps', pass: report.comparison.stepA === report.comparison.stepB });
const snapshot = takeSnapshot(simulations[0], 'main');
simulations[0].rules.align = 0; stepSim(simulations[0], 1);
report.checks.push({ name: 'Deep immutable snapshot', pass: Object.isFrozen(snapshot.particles[0]) && hashParticles(snapshot.particles) === hashes[0] });
const restored = validateDocument({ schemaVersion: 1, snapshot, preset: 'SILENCE', edit: { ...DEFAULT_EDIT } });
report.checks.push({ name: 'Validated coordinate roundtrip', pass: restored.snapshot.hash === snapshot.hash && JSON.stringify(restored.snapshot.particles) === JSON.stringify(snapshot.particles) });
report.summary = { pass: report.checks.filter(x => x.pass).length, fail: report.checks.filter(x => !x.pass).length };
fs.writeFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL('./node-results.json', import.meta.url)), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
console.log(JSON.stringify(report)); if (report.summary.fail) process.exitCode = 1;

    for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
      await page.evaluate(preset => __EC.setPreset(preset), preset);
      const png = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.exportPNG({ download: false }));
      const snapshot = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.snapshotHash());
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, `poster-${preset.toLowerCase()}.png`), Buffer.from(png.dataURL.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
      posterData[preset] = { width: png.width, height: png.height, hash: hash(png.dataURL), snapshot };
      await shot(`1440x900-${preset.toLowerCase()}`);
    }
    check('Three posters: same snapshot, distinct pixels', new Set(Object.values(posterData).map(x => x.snapshot)).size === 1 && new Set(Object.values(posterData).map(x => x.hash)).size === 3, posterData);
    check('PNG 1800x2400', Object.values(posterData).every(x => x.width === 1800 && x.height === 2400), posterData);
    const contact = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 1350; canvas.height = 600;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        __EC.setPreset(['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL'][i]);
        const p = await __EC.exportPNG({ download: false }); const image = new Image(); image.src = p.dataURL; await image.decode();
        ctx.drawImage(image, i * 450, 0, 450, 600);
      }
      return canvas.toDataURL();
    });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'contact-sheet.png'), Buffer.from(contact.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
    const roundTrip = await page.evaluate(() => {
      __EC.setEdit({ keep: 0.63, focusX: 0.42, focusY: 0.61, focusAmt: 0.7, space: 0.55, trace: 0.44, typeOn: true, title: '同じ/世界', sub: 'SAME PARTICLES. MY DECISIONS.' });
      const before = __EC.exportJSON(); __EC.reset(); const loaded = __EC.loadJSON(JSON.stringify(before)); const after = __EC.exportJSON();
      const canonical = value => value && typeof value === 'object' ? Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(canonical) : Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map(k => [k, canonical(value[k])])) : value;
      return { before, after, loaded, equal: JSON.stringify(canonical(before)) === JSON.stringify(canonical(after)) };
    });
    check('JSON round trip exact snapshot / edit / title', roundTrip.loaded.ok && roundTrip.equal, { before: roundTrip.before.snapshot.hash, after: roundTrip.after.snapshot.hash, edit: roundTrip.after.edit });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'reproduction.json'), JSON.stringify(roundTrip.before, null, 2) + '\n');
    const invalid = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const good = __EC.exportJSON(), results = [];
      const cases = [ ['broken', '{'], ['oversized', ' '.repeat(2 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)], ['NaN', { ...good, edit: { ...good.edit, keep: NaN } }],
        ['version', { ...good, schemaVersion: 9 }], ['range', { ...good, edit: { ...good.edit, focusX: 2 } }],
        ['missing', { schemaVersion: 1 }], ['hash', { ...good, snapshot: { ...good.snapshot, hash: 'bad' } }] ];
      for (const [name, input] of cases) {
        const result = __EC.loadJSON(input);
        results.push({ name, ...result, ui: document.getElementById('status').textContent, unchanged: __EC.snapshotHash() === good.snapshot.hash });
      }
      return results;
    });
    for (const x of invalid) check('Reject ' + x.name + ' with UI reason', !x.ok && x.error === x.ui && x.unchanged, x);
    await shot('json-validation-error');
    const unknown = await page.evaluate(() => { const doc = __EC.exportJSON(); doc.unknown = { harmless: true }; doc.edit.unknown = 4; const result = __EC.loadJSON(doc); return { result, ignored: !('unknown' in __EC.exportJSON()) && !('unknown' in __EC.getState().edit) }; });
    check('Unknown keys ignored', unknown.result.ok && unknown.ignored, unknown);
    const textSafety = await page.evaluate(() => {
      __EC.setEdit({ title: '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>'.repeat(10), sub: '長'.repeat(1000) });
      return { title: __EC.getState().edit.title, subLength: Array.from(__EC.getState().edit.sub).length, injected: document.querySelector('img[src="x"]') !== null };
    });
    check('Text safety and truncation', !textSafety.injected && Array.from(textSafety.title).length === 32 && textSafety.subLength === 80, textSafety);
    await page.evaluate(doc => { __EC.loadJSON(doc); __EC.setEdit({ title: 'EMER/GENCE', sub: 'A FLOCK BECOMES A COMPOSITION.' }); __EC.setPreset('SIGNAL'); }, roundTrip.before);
    // Independent geometry checks include extreme SPACE / TRACE and long text.
    const geometry = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const { compositionGeometry, FRAMES } = window.EC.composition;
      const results = [];
      for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) for (const space of [0, 1]) for (const trace of [0, 1]) {
        const doc = __EC.exportJSON(); doc.preset = preset; doc.edit = { ...doc.edit, keep: 1, space, trace };
        const g = compositionGeometry(doc.snapshot, doc.edit, preset, 1800, 2400);
        const [x0, y0, x1, y1] = FRAMES[preset].box, e = 0.5;
        results.push({ preset, space, trace, inside: g.points.every(p => [p.x - p.radius, p.x - p.dx - p.radius].every(x => x >= x0 * 1800 - e) && [p.x + p.radius, p.x - p.dx + p.radius].every(x => x <= x1 * 1800 + e) && [p.y - p.radius, p.y - p.dy - p.radius].every(y => y >= y0 * 2400 - e) && [p.y + p.radius, p.y - p.dy + p.radius].every(y => y <= y1 * 2400 + e)) });
      }
      return results;
    });
    check('No particle/trace cropping at extreme edit settings', geometry.every(x => x.inside), geometry);
    const editContract = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const { compositionGeometry } = window.EC.composition;
      const doc = __EC.exportJSON(), g = edit => compositionGeometry(doc.snapshot, { ...doc.edit, ...edit }, 'SIGNAL', 1800, 2400);
      const sparse = g({ keep: 0.2 }), dense = g({ keep: 0.8 });
      const noTrace = g({ trace: 0 }), fullTrace = g({ trace: 1 });
      const spaces = [0, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1].map(space => g({ space }).scale);
      return { nestedIDs: sparse.points.filter(p => p.visible).every(p => dense.points[p.id].visible),
        tracePositions: noTrace.points.every((p, i) => p.x === fullTrace.points[i].x && p.y === fullTrace.points[i].y),
        traceDirections: fullTrace.points.every((p, i) => { const v = doc.snapshot.particles[i]; return Math.abs(p.dx * v.vy - p.dy * v.vx) < 1e-12; }),
        spaces, monotonicSpace: spaces.every((x, i) => i === 0 || x < spaces[i - 1]) };
    });
    check('KEEP nested IDs, TRACE actual velocities without moving dots, SPACE monotonic', editContract.nestedIDs && editContract.tracePositions && editContract.traceDirections && editContract.monotonicSpace, editContract);
    const rendererText = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const { renderComposition } = window.EC.render; const results = [];
      for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) for (const title of ['EMER/GENCE', '漢'.repeat(32), 'W'.repeat(32)]) {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 1800; canvas.height = 2400;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'), texts = [], original = ctx.fillText.bind(ctx);
        ctx.fillText = (text, x, y, ...args) => { const m = ctx.measureText(text); texts.push({ text, left: x - m.actualBoundingBoxLeft, right: x + m.actualBoundingBoxRight, top: y - m.actualBoundingBoxAscent, bottom: y + m.actualBoundingBoxDescent }); original(text, x, y, ...args); };

+// Run only before v0.3 app edits. Refuses to replace the fixed baseline.
+const { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+const crypto = require('node:crypto');
+const out = path.join(__dirname, 'baseline');
+async function run() {
+  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'))) throw Error('Baseline already fixed');
+  const hashes = {};
+  for (const file of ['index.html', 'styles.css', ...fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'js')).map(f => 'js/' + f)]) {
+    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, file));
+    if (!bytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, '_backup_v02', file)))) throw Error('Backup mismatch: ' + file);
+    hashes[file] = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+  }
+  // Preserve the prior run's artifacts before any regression runner can write results.
+  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'verification'))) {
+    if (name === 'v03' || name === 'claude-review' || name === 'browser-temp') continue;
+    fs.cpSync(path.join(app, 'verification', name), path.join(out, 'previous', name), { recursive: true });
+  }
+  const runtime = await launch();
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url); await page.evaluate(() => __EC.pause(true));
+    await page.locator('#begin').click();
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.step(600); });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'rule.png'), fullPage: true });
+    const data = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); return { deterministic: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600), stats: __EC.compareStats(), view: __EC.getState().view }; });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'compare.png'), fullPage: true });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE'); });
+    data.document = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.exportJSON());
+    for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
+      saveImage(path.join(out, preset + '.png'), await page.evaluate(async p => { __EC.setPreset(p); return (await __EC.exportPNG({ download: false })).dataURL; }, preset));
+    }
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'edit.png'), fullPage: true });
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'), JSON.stringify({ browser: runtime.browser.version(), hashes, ...data }, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ deterministic: data.deterministic, stats: data.stats, backupFiles: Object.keys(hashes).length }));
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const app = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
+async function launch() {

+  return { browser, async close() { await browser.close(); if (path.resolve(temp).startsWith(__dirname + path.sep)) fs.rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true }); } };

+// Run only before v0.3 app edits. Refuses to replace the fixed baseline.
+const { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+const crypto = require('node:crypto');
+const out = path.join(__dirname, 'baseline');
+async function run() {
+  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'))) throw Error('Baseline already fixed');
+  const hashes = {};
+  for (const file of ['index.html', 'styles.css', ...fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'js')).map(f => 'js/' + f)]) {
+    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, file));
+    if (!bytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, '_backup_v02', file)))) throw Error('Backup mismatch: ' + file);
+    hashes[file] = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+  }
+  // Preserve the prior run's artifacts before any regression runner can write results.
+  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'verification'))) {
+    if (name === 'v03' || name === 'claude-review' || name === 'browser-temp') continue;
+    fs.cpSync(path.join(app, 'verification', name), path.join(out, 'previous', name), { recursive: true });
+  }
+  const runtime = await launch();
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url); await page.evaluate(() => __EC.pause(true));
+    await page.locator('#begin').click();
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.step(600); });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'rule.png'), fullPage: true });
+    const data = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); return { deterministic: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600), stats: __EC.compareStats(), view: __EC.getState().view }; });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'compare.png'), fullPage: true });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE'); });
+    data.document = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.exportJSON());
+    for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
+      saveImage(path.join(out, preset + '.png'), await page.evaluate(async p => { __EC.setPreset(p); return (await __EC.exportPNG({ download: false })).dataURL; }, preset));
+    }
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'edit.png'), fullPage: true });
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'), JSON.stringify({ browser: runtime.browser.version(), hashes, ...data }, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ deterministic: data.deterministic, stats: data.stats, backupFiles: Object.keys(hashes).length }));
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const fs = require('node:fs'), path = require('node:path');
+const { pathToFileURL } = require('node:url');
+const app = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
+async function launch() {
+  const temp = path.join(__dirname, 'browser-temp');
+  fs.mkdirSync(temp, { recursive: true });
+  process.env.TEMP = temp; process.env.TMP = temp;
+  const { chromium } = require(process.env.EC_PLAYWRIGHT || 'C:/Users/so173/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/playwright');
+  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, executablePath: process.env.EC_CHROME || 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', args: ['--disable-gpu'] });
+  return { browser, async close() { await browser.close(); if (path.resolve(temp).startsWith(__dirname + path.sep)) fs.rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true }); } };
+}
+const saveImage = (file, url) => fs.writeFileSync(file, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
+module.exports = { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url: pathToFileURL(path.join(app, 'index.html')).href };

+const { fs, path, launch, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+async function run() {
+  const runtime = await launch(), checks = [];
+  const check = (name, pass, evidence) => checks.push({ name, pass, evidence });
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url);
+    for (const [width, height] of [[1440, 900], [1280, 800], [390, 844], [844, 390]]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height });
+      await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.compare(true); });
+      await page.waitForTimeout(80);
+      const initial = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const c = document.getElementById('world'), ctx = c.getContext('2d'), half = c.width / 2;
+        const a = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, half, c.height).data, b = ctx.getImageData(half, 0, half, c.height).data;
+        let diff = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) diff++;
+        const view = __EC.getState().view;
+        const simA = EC.sim.createSim(42), simB = EC.sim.createSim(42, { ...EC.sim.DEFAULT_RULES, alignOn: false });
+        const box = EC.render.worldBox(half, c.height);
+        return { diff, half, view, sameWorld: JSON.stringify(simA.particles) === JSON.stringify(simB.particles),
+          sameProjection: JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simA, box, view.A)) === JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simB, box, view.B)) };
+      });
+      check(`${width}x${height} LOCK step0 identical world / projection / pixels`, initial.diff === 0 && initial.sameWorld && initial.sameProjection && JSON.stringify(initial.view.A) === JSON.stringify(initial.view.B), initial);
+    }
+    const invariant = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      __EC.step(600); __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE');
+      const before = { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() };
+      const views = [];
+      for (const mode of ['FOLLOW', 'LOCK']) for (const z of [1, 3, 1.8]) { __EC.setViewMode(mode); __EC.setZoom(z); views.push(__EC.getState().view); }
+      return { before, after: { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() }, views, hash: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600) };
+    });
+    const baseline = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'baseline/baseline.json')));
+    check('step600 hash and Phi match live v02 baseline', invariant.hash === '5828ce7f' && JSON.stringify(invariant.before.stats) === JSON.stringify(baseline.stats), invariant.before.stats);
+    check('LOCK FOLLOW ZOOM preserve sim stats and artwork document', JSON.stringify(invariant.before) === JSON.stringify(invariant.after), { views: invariant.views });
+    check('FOLLOW keeps independent tracking', invariant.views[0].A.camX !== invariant.views[0].B.camX || invariant.views[0].A.camY !== invariant.views[0].B.camY);
+    const report = { browser: runtime.browser.version(), checks, pass: checks.filter(c => c.pass).length, fail: checks.filter(c => !c.pass).length };
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'causal-results.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(report)); if (report.fail) process.exitCode = 1;
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const worldBuffers = new WeakMap();
 function renderWorld(ctx, sim, box, view) {
+  // Rasterize each world's local coordinates before placing its panel. This avoids
+  // translated-path antialias rounding making identical A/B worlds look different.
+  if (!worldBuffers.has(ctx)) worldBuffers.set(ctx, document.createElement('canvas'));
+  const buffer = worldBuffers.get(ctx), dpr = Math.abs(ctx.getTransform().a) || 1;
+  const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(box.width * dpr)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(box.height * dpr));
+  if (buffer.width !== w || buffer.height !== h) { buffer.width = w; buffer.height = h; }
+  const local = buffer.getContext('2d'); local.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
+  local.clearRect(0, 0, box.width, box.height);
+  drawWorld(local, sim, { ...box, x: 0, y: 0 }, view);
+  ctx.drawImage(buffer, box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height);
+}

+// Run only before v0.3 app edits. Refuses to replace the fixed baseline.
+const { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+const crypto = require('node:crypto');
+const out = path.join(__dirname, 'baseline');
+async function run() {
+  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'))) throw Error('Baseline already fixed');
+  const hashes = {};
+  for (const file of ['index.html', 'styles.css', ...fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'js')).map(f => 'js/' + f)]) {
+    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, file));
+    if (!bytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, '_backup_v02', file)))) throw Error('Backup mismatch: ' + file);
+    hashes[file] = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+  }
+  // Preserve the prior run's artifacts before any regression runner can write results.
+  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'verification'))) {
+    if (name === 'v03' || name === 'claude-review' || name === 'browser-temp') continue;
+    fs.cpSync(path.join(app, 'verification', name), path.join(out, 'previous', name), { recursive: true });
+  }
+  const runtime = await launch();
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url); await page.evaluate(() => __EC.pause(true));
+    await page.locator('#begin').click();
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.step(600); });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'rule.png'), fullPage: true });
+    const data = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); return { deterministic: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600), stats: __EC.compareStats(), view: __EC.getState().view }; });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'compare.png'), fullPage: true });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE'); });
+    data.document = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.exportJSON());
+    for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
+      saveImage(path.join(out, preset + '.png'), await page.evaluate(async p => { __EC.setPreset(p); return (await __EC.exportPNG({ download: false })).dataURL; }, preset));
+    }
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'edit.png'), fullPage: true });
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'), JSON.stringify({ browser: runtime.browser.version(), hashes, ...data }, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ deterministic: data.deterministic, stats: data.stats, backupFiles: Object.keys(hashes).length }));
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const fs = require('node:fs'), path = require('node:path');
+const { pathToFileURL } = require('node:url');
+const app = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
+async function launch() {
+  const temp = path.join(__dirname, 'browser-temp');
+  fs.mkdirSync(temp, { recursive: true });
+  process.env.TEMP = temp; process.env.TMP = temp;
+  const { chromium } = require(process.env.EC_PLAYWRIGHT || 'C:/Users/so173/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/playwright');
+  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, executablePath: process.env.EC_CHROME || 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', args: ['--disable-gpu'] });
+  return { browser, async close() { await browser.close(); if (path.resolve(temp).startsWith(__dirname + path.sep)) fs.rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true }); } };
+}

+const { fs, path, launch, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+async function run() {
+  const runtime = await launch(), checks = [];
+  const check = (name, pass, evidence) => checks.push({ name, pass, evidence });
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url);
+    for (const [width, height] of [[1440, 900], [1280, 800], [390, 844], [844, 390]]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height });
+      await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.compare(true); });
+      await page.waitForTimeout(80);
+      const initial = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const c = document.getElementById('world'), ctx = c.getContext('2d'), half = c.width / 2;
+        const a = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, half, c.height).data, b = ctx.getImageData(half, 0, half, c.height).data;
+        let diff = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) diff++;
+        const view = __EC.getState().view;
+        const simA = EC.sim.createSim(42), simB = EC.sim.createSim(42, { ...EC.sim.DEFAULT_RULES, alignOn: false });
+        const box = EC.render.worldBox(half, c.height);
+        return { diff, half, view, sameWorld: JSON.stringify(simA.particles) === JSON.stringify(simB.particles),
+          sameProjection: JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simA, box, view.A)) === JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simB, box, view.B)) };
+      });
+      check(`${width}x${height} LOCK step0 identical world / projection / pixels`, initial.diff === 0 && initial.sameWorld && initial.sameProjection && JSON.stringify(initial.view.A) === JSON.stringify(initial.view.B), initial);
+    }
+    const invariant = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      __EC.step(600); __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE');
+      const before = { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() };
+      const views = [];
+      for (const mode of ['FOLLOW', 'LOCK']) for (const z of [1, 3, 1.8]) { __EC.setViewMode(mode); __EC.setZoom(z); views.push(__EC.getState().view); }
+      return { before, after: { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() }, views, hash: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600) };
+    });
+    const baseline = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'baseline/baseline.json')));
+    check('step600 hash and Phi match live v02 baseline', invariant.hash === '5828ce7f' && JSON.stringify(invariant.before.stats) === JSON.stringify(baseline.stats), invariant.before.stats);
+    check('LOCK FOLLOW ZOOM preserve sim stats and artwork document', JSON.stringify(invariant.before) === JSON.stringify(invariant.after), { views: invariant.views });
+    check('FOLLOW keeps independent tracking', invariant.views[0].A.camX !== invariant.views[0].B.camX || invariant.views[0].A.camY !== invariant.views[0].B.camY);
+    const report = { browser: runtime.browser.version(), checks, pass: checks.filter(c => c.pass).length, fail: checks.filter(c => !c.pass).length };
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'causal-results.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(report)); if (report.fail) process.exitCode = 1;
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const worldBuffers = new WeakMap();
 function renderWorld(ctx, sim, box, view) {
+  // Rasterize each world's local coordinates before placing its panel. This avoids
+  // translated-path antialias rounding making identical A/B worlds look different.
+  if (!worldBuffers.has(ctx)) worldBuffers.set(ctx, document.createElement('canvas'));
+  const buffer = worldBuffers.get(ctx), dpr = Math.abs(ctx.getTransform().a) || 1;
+  const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(box.width * dpr)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(box.height * dpr));
+  if (buffer.width !== w || buffer.height !== h) { buffer.width = w; buffer.height = h; }
+  const local = buffer.getContext('2d'); local.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
+  local.clearRect(0, 0, box.width, box.height);
+  drawWorld(local, sim, { ...box, x: 0, y: 0 }, view);
+  ctx.drawImage(buffer, box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height);

+// Run only before v0.3 app edits. Refuses to replace the fixed baseline.
+const { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+const crypto = require('node:crypto');
+const out = path.join(__dirname, 'baseline');
+async function run() {
+  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'))) throw Error('Baseline already fixed');
+  const hashes = {};
+  for (const file of ['index.html', 'styles.css', ...fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'js')).map(f => 'js/' + f)]) {
+    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, file));
+    if (!bytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, '_backup_v02', file)))) throw Error('Backup mismatch: ' + file);
+    hashes[file] = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+  }
+  // Preserve the prior run's artifacts before any regression runner can write results.
+  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'verification'))) {
+    if (name === 'v03' || name === 'claude-review' || name === 'browser-temp') continue;
+    fs.cpSync(path.join(app, 'verification', name), path.join(out, 'previous', name), { recursive: true });
+  }
+  const runtime = await launch();
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url); await page.evaluate(() => __EC.pause(true));
+    await page.locator('#begin').click();
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.step(600); });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'rule.png'), fullPage: true });
+    const data = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); return { deterministic: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600), stats: __EC.compareStats(), view: __EC.getState().view }; });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'compare.png'), fullPage: true });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE'); });
+    data.document = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.exportJSON());
+    for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
+      saveImage(path.join(out, preset + '.png'), await page.evaluate(async p => { __EC.setPreset(p); return (await __EC.exportPNG({ download: false })).dataURL; }, preset));
+    }
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'edit.png'), fullPage: true });
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'), JSON.stringify({ browser: runtime.browser.version(), hashes, ...data }, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ deterministic: data.deterministic, stats: data.stats, backupFiles: Object.keys(hashes).length }));
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const { pathToFileURL } = require('node:url');
+const app = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
+async function launch() {
+  const temp = path.join(__dirname, 'browser-temp');

+  process.env.TEMP = temp; process.env.TMP = temp;
+  const { chromium } = require(process.env.EC_PLAYWRIGHT || 'C:/Users/so173/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/playwright');
+  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, executablePath: process.env.EC_CHROME || 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', args: ['--disable-gpu'] });
+  return { browser, async close() { await browser.close(); if (path.resolve(temp).startsWith(__dirname + path.sep)) fs.rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true }); } };
+}

+const { fs, path, launch, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+async function run() {
+  const runtime = await launch(), checks = [];
+  const check = (name, pass, evidence) => checks.push({ name, pass, evidence });
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url);
+    for (const [width, height] of [[1440, 900], [1280, 800], [390, 844], [844, 390]]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height });
+      await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.compare(true); });
+      await page.waitForTimeout(80);
+      const initial = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const c = document.getElementById('world'), ctx = c.getContext('2d'), half = c.width / 2;
+        const a = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, half, c.height).data, b = ctx.getImageData(half, 0, half, c.height).data;
+        let diff = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) diff++;
+        const view = __EC.getState().view;
+        const simA = EC.sim.createSim(42), simB = EC.sim.createSim(42, { ...EC.sim.DEFAULT_RULES, alignOn: false });
+        const box = EC.render.worldBox(half, c.height);
+        return { diff, half, view, sameWorld: JSON.stringify(simA.particles) === JSON.stringify(simB.particles),
+          sameProjection: JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simA, box, view.A)) === JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simB, box, view.B)) };
+      });
+      check(`${width}x${height} LOCK step0 identical world / projection / pixels`, initial.diff === 0 && initial.sameWorld && initial.sameProjection && JSON.stringify(initial.view.A) === JSON.stringify(initial.view.B), initial);
+    }
+    const invariant = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      __EC.step(600); __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE');
+      const before = { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() };
+      const views = [];
+      for (const mode of ['FOLLOW', 'LOCK']) for (const z of [1, 3, 1.8]) { __EC.setViewMode(mode); __EC.setZoom(z); views.push(__EC.getState().view); }
+      return { before, after: { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() }, views, hash: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600) };
+    });
+    const baseline = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'baseline/baseline.json')));
+    check('step600 hash and Phi match live v02 baseline', invariant.hash === '5828ce7f' && JSON.stringify(invariant.before.stats) === JSON.stringify(baseline.stats), invariant.before.stats);
+    check('LOCK FOLLOW ZOOM preserve sim stats and artwork document', JSON.stringify(invariant.before) === JSON.stringify(invariant.after), { views: invariant.views });
+    check('FOLLOW keeps independent tracking', invariant.views[0].A.camX !== invariant.views[0].B.camX || invariant.views[0].A.camY !== invariant.views[0].B.camY);
+    const report = { browser: runtime.browser.version(), checks, pass: checks.filter(c => c.pass).length, fail: checks.filter(c => !c.pass).length };
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'causal-results.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(report)); if (report.fail) process.exitCode = 1;
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

  const pw = Math.max(1, Math.round(box.width * dpr)), ph = Math.max(1, Math.round(box.height * dpr));
  if (buffer.width "'!== pw || buffer.height !== ph) { buffer.width = pw; buffer.height = ph; redraw = true; }
  if (redraw) renderComposition(state, buffer);
  ctx.drawImage(buffer, box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height);
}
const clamp01 = n => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
const smooth = t => t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
function freezeTiming(progress) {
  return { move: smooth(clamp01((progress - 0.15) / 0.55)), print: smooth(clamp01((progress - 0.88) / 0.12)),
    beat: progress <= 0.15 ? '"'HOLD' : progress < 0.70 ? 'TRANSFER' : progress < 0.88 ? 'STILL' : 'PRINT' };
}
// One primary per real ID. Copies converge and fade; selection occurs only at PRINT.
function freezeGeometry(state, width, height, source, progress) {
  const box = paperBox(width, height), timing = freezeTiming(progress);
  const g = compositionGeometry(state.snapshot, state.edit, state.preset, box.width, box.height);
  const { move, print } = timing;
  const points = source.map(src => {
    const p = g.points[src.id];
    return { id: src.id, primary: src.primary,
      x: src.x * width + (box.x + p.x - src.x * width) * move,
      y: src.y * height + (box.y + p.y - src.y * height) * move,
      dx: src.dx * width + (p.dx - src.dx * width) * move,
      dy: src.dy * height + (p.dy - src.dy * height) * move,
      radius: src.radius + (p.radius - src.radius) * move,
      alpha: (1 + ((p.visible ? p.alpha : 0) - 1) * print) * (src.primary ? 1 : 1 - move),
      lineWidth: src.radius * 0.65 + (box.width * 0.0009 - src.radius * 0.65) * move };
  });
  return { box, timing, points, composition: g };
}
function renderFreeze(ctx, state, width, height, source, progress, from = null) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.noir; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
  if (progress >= 1) { renderPaper(ctx, state, width, height); return; }
  const { box, timing, points, composition } = freezeGeometry(state, width, height, source, progress);
  const { move, print } = timing;
  const f = from ? { x: from.x * width, y: from.y * height, width: from.w * width, height: from.h * height } : box;
  if (progress <= 0.15 && from?.view) {
    // Exactly the sampled screen, stationary for 172.5 ms. No overlay shapes.
    renderWorld(ctx, { particles: state.snapshot.particles }, { ...f, size: Math.min(f.width, f.height) }, from.view);
    return;
  }
  const area = { x: f.x + (box.x - f.x) * move, y: f.y + (box.y - f.y) * move,
    width: f.width + (box.width - f.width) * move, height: f.height + (box.height - f.height) * move };
  const dark = state.preset === 'CURRENT';
  // A hard printing front changes paper AND ink in the same spatial region.
  // Unlike interpolated greys, neither side ever loses particle/background contrast.
  const front = box.y + box.height * print;
  if ("'!dark && print > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.paper; ctx.fillRect(box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height * print); }
  function drawPass(ink, top, bottom) {
    ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(area.x, top, area.width, Math.max(0, bottom - top)); ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = ink; ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.lineCap = '"'round';
    for (const p of points) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha * (0.42 + 0.58 * move); ctx.lineWidth = p.lineWidth;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.lineTo(p.x - p.dx, p.y - p.dy); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha; dot(ctx, p.x, p.y, p.radius);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  if ("'!dark && print > 0) {
    drawPass(COLORS.ink, area.y, front); drawPass(COLORS.paper, front, area.y + area.height);
  } else drawPass(COLORS.paper, area.y, area.y + area.height);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  if (print > 0) {
    if (state.preset === '"'SIGNAL' && composition.focus) {
      ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.yellow; ctx.globalAlpha = print;
      dot(ctx, box.x + composition.focus.x, box.y + composition.focus.y, box.width * (0.006 + state.edit.focusAmt * 0.008));
    }
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(box.x, box.y); ctx.globalAlpha = print; drawTypography(ctx, state, box.width, box.height); ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
"'` + s.slice(end);

+// Run only before v0.3 app edits. Refuses to replace the fixed baseline.
+const { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+const crypto = require('node:crypto');
+const out = path.join(__dirname, 'baseline');
+async function run() {
+  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'))) throw Error('Baseline already fixed');
+  const hashes = {};
+  for (const file of ['index.html', 'styles.css', ...fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'js')).map(f => 'js/' + f)]) {
+    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, file));
+    if (!bytes.equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, '_backup_v02', file)))) throw Error('Backup mismatch: ' + file);
+    hashes[file] = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+  }
+  // Preserve the prior run's artifacts before any regression runner can write results.
+  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(path.join(app, 'verification'))) {
+    if (name === 'v03' || name === 'claude-review' || name === 'browser-temp') continue;
+    fs.cpSync(path.join(app, 'verification', name), path.join(out, 'previous', name), { recursive: true });
+  }
+  const runtime = await launch();
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url); await page.evaluate(() => __EC.pause(true));
+    await page.locator('#begin').click();
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.step(600); });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'rule.png'), fullPage: true });
+    const data = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); return { deterministic: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600), stats: __EC.compareStats(), view: __EC.getState().view }; });
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'compare.png'), fullPage: true });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE'); });
+    data.document = await page.evaluate(() => __EC.exportJSON());
+    for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
+      saveImage(path.join(out, preset + '.png'), await page.evaluate(async p => { __EC.setPreset(p); return (await __EC.exportPNG({ download: false })).dataURL; }, preset));
+    }
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'edit.png'), fullPage: true });
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'baseline.json'), JSON.stringify({ browser: runtime.browser.version(), hashes, ...data }, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ deterministic: data.deterministic, stats: data.stats, backupFiles: Object.keys(hashes).length }));
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const fs = require('node:fs'), path = require('node:path');
+const { pathToFileURL } = require('node:url');
+const app = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
+async function launch() {
+  const temp = path.join(__dirname, 'browser-temp');
+  fs.mkdirSync(temp, { recursive: true });
+  process.env.TEMP = temp; process.env.TMP = temp;
+  const { chromium } = require(process.env.EC_PLAYWRIGHT || 'C:/Users/so173/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/playwright');
+  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, executablePath: process.env.EC_CHROME || 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', args: ['--disable-gpu'] });
+  return { browser, async close() { await browser.close(); if (path.resolve(temp).startsWith(__dirname + path.sep)) fs.rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true }); } };
+}

+module.exports = { fs, path, app, launch, saveImage, url: pathToFileURL(path.join(app, 'index.html')).href };

+const { fs, path, launch, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+async function run() {
+  const runtime = await launch(), checks = [];
+  const check = (name, pass, evidence) => checks.push({ name, pass, evidence });
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
+    await page.goto(url);
+    for (const [width, height] of [[1440, 900], [1280, 800], [390, 844], [844, 390]]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height });
+      await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.compare(true); });
+      await page.waitForTimeout(80);
+      const initial = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const c = document.getElementById('world'), ctx = c.getContext('2d'), half = c.width / 2;
+        const a = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, half, c.height).data, b = ctx.getImageData(half, 0, half, c.height).data;
+        let diff = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) diff++;
+        const view = __EC.getState().view;
+        const simA = EC.sim.createSim(42), simB = EC.sim.createSim(42, { ...EC.sim.DEFAULT_RULES, alignOn: false });
+        const box = EC.render.worldBox(half, c.height);
+        return { diff, half, view, sameWorld: JSON.stringify(simA.particles) === JSON.stringify(simB.particles),
+          sameProjection: JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simA, box, view.A)) === JSON.stringify(EC.render.worldPoints(simB, box, view.B)) };
+      });
+      check(`${width}x${height} LOCK step0 identical world / projection / pixels`, initial.diff === 0 && initial.sameWorld && initial.sameProjection && JSON.stringify(initial.view.A) === JSON.stringify(initial.view.B), initial);
+    }
+    const invariant = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      __EC.step(600); __EC.take('A'); __EC.setPreset('SILENCE');
+      const before = { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() };
+      const views = [];
+      for (const mode of ['FOLLOW', 'LOCK']) for (const z of [1, 3, 1.8]) { __EC.setViewMode(mode); __EC.setZoom(z); views.push(__EC.getState().view); }
+      return { before, after: { stats: __EC.compareStats(), doc: __EC.exportJSON() }, views, hash: __EC.simDeterminism(42, 600) };
+    });
+    const baseline = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'baseline/baseline.json')));
+    check('step600 hash and Phi match live v02 baseline', invariant.hash === '5828ce7f' && JSON.stringify(invariant.before.stats) === JSON.stringify(baseline.stats), invariant.before.stats);
+    check('LOCK FOLLOW ZOOM preserve sim stats and artwork document', JSON.stringify(invariant.before) === JSON.stringify(invariant.after), { views: invariant.views });
+    check('FOLLOW keeps independent tracking', invariant.views[0].A.camX !== invariant.views[0].B.camX || invariant.views[0].A.camY !== invariant.views[0].B.camY);
+    const report = { browser: runtime.browser.version(), checks, pass: checks.filter(c => c.pass).length, fail: checks.filter(c => !c.pass).length };
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'causal-results.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(report)); if (report.fail) process.exitCode = 1;
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const { fs, path, launch, saveImage, url } = require('./runtime.cjs');
+async function run() {
+  const runtime = await launch(), checks = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await runtime.browser.newPage(); await page.goto(url);
+    for (const [label, width, height] of [['desktop', 1440, 900], ['mobile', 390, 844]]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height }); await page.waitForTimeout(70);
+      for (const preset of ['SILENCE', 'CURRENT', 'SIGNAL']) {
+        const result = await page.evaluate(preset => {
+          __EC.pause(true); __EC.setSeed(42); __EC.compare(true); __EC.step(600); __EC.setPreset(preset);
+          __EC.take('B');
+          const doc = __EC.exportJSON(), tr = __EC.diagnostics().transition;
+          const c = document.getElementById('world'), w = c.getBoundingClientRect().width, h = c.getBoundingClientRect().height;
+          const make = () => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = Math.round(w); c.height = Math.round(h); return c; };
+          const a = make(), b = make(), ca = a.getContext('2d'), cb = b.getContext('2d');
+          const diff = () => { const aa = ca.getImageData(0, 0, a.width, a.height).data, bb = cb.getImageData(0, 0, b.width, b.height).data; let n = 0; for (let i = 0; i < aa.length; i++) if (aa[i] !== bb[i]) n++; return n; };
+          ca.fillStyle = '#070707'; ca.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
+          EC.render.renderWorld(ca, { particles: doc.snapshot.particles }, EC.render.worldBox(w, h, 'B', true), tr.from.view);
+          EC.render.renderFreeze(cb, doc, w, h, tr.source, 0, tr.from);
+          const startDiff = diff();
+          EC.render.renderFreeze(cb, doc, w, h, tr.source, 0.15, tr.from);
+          const holdDiff = diff();
+          const box = EC.render.paperBox(w, h), paper = document.createElement('canvas'); paper.width = Math.round(box.width); paper.height = Math.round(box.height);
+          EC.render.renderComposition(doc, paper); ca.fillStyle = '#070707'; ca.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); ca.drawImage(paper, box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height);
+          EC.render.renderFreeze(cb, doc, w, h, tr.source, 1, tr.from);
+          const endDiff = diff();
+          const percentages = [0, 15, 35, 60, 80, 90, 100], frames = [];
+          const strip = document.createElement('canvas'), tileW = labelWidth = Math.round(w * (w > 600 ? 0.4 : 0.65)), tileH = Math.round(h * tileW / w);
+          strip.width = tileW * 7; strip.height = tileH + 34;
+          const sc = strip.getContext('2d'); sc.fillStyle = '#070707'; sc.fillRect(0, 0, strip.width, strip.height);
+          percentages.forEach((pct, i) => {
+            const p = pct / 100, g = EC.render.freezeGeometry(doc, w, h, tr.source, p);
+            const primary = g.points.filter(q => q.primary);
+            EC.render.renderFreeze(cb, doc, w, h, tr.source, p, tr.from);
+            sc.drawImage(b, i * tileW, 34, tileW, tileH); sc.fillStyle = '#f2f2ef'; sc.font = '13px monospace'; sc.fillText(`${pct}% ${g.timing.beat}`, i * tileW + 8, 22);
+            frames.push({ pct, ids: primary.map(q => q.id), positiveAlpha: primary.filter(q => q.alpha > 0).length,
+              mapping: primary.every(q => { const src = tr.source.find(s => s.primary && s.id === q.id), target = g.composition.points[q.id]; return Math.abs(q.x - (src.x * w + (g.box.x + target.x - src.x * w) * g.timing.move)) < 1e-9; }) });
+          });
+          const stillA = EC.render.freezeGeometry(doc, w, h, tr.source, 0.70), stillB = EC.render.freezeGeometry(doc, w, h, tr.source, 0.88);
+          return { startDiff, holdDiff, endDiff, duration: tr.duration, frames, still: JSON.stringify(stillA.points) === JSON.stringify(stillB.points), image: strip.toDataURL() };
+        }, preset);
+        saveImage(path.join(__dirname, `freeze_strip_${label}_${preset.toLowerCase()}.png`), result.image); delete result.image;
+        checks.push({ name: `${label} ${preset} source / hold / endpoint pixels, 600 IDs, mapping, quiet`, pass: result.startDiff === 0 && result.holdDiff === 0 && result.endDiff === 0 && result.duration <= 1150 && result.still && result.frames.every(f => f.ids.length === 600 && new Set(f.ids).size === 600 && f.mapping && (f.pct === 100 || f.positiveAlpha === 600)), evidence: result });
+      }
+    }
+    await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' }); await page.reload();
+    const rm = await page.evaluate(() => { __EC.compare(true); __EC.take('A'); return { duration: __EC.diagnostics().transition.duration, hash: __EC.snapshotHash() }; });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => __EC.getState().phase === 'EDIT');
+    checks.push({ name: 'reduced motion 100 ms keeps hash', pass: rm.duration === 100 && rm.hash === await page.evaluate(() => __EC.snapshotHash()), evidence: rm });
+    const report = { checks, pass: checks.filter(c => c.pass).length, fail: checks.filter(c => !c.pass).length };
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'freeze-results.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ pass: report.pass, fail: report.fail, results: checks.map(c => ({ name: c.name, pass: c.pass, start: c.evidence.startDiff, end: c.evidence.endDiff })) })); if (report.fail) process.exitCode = 1;
+  } finally { await runtime.close(); }
+}
+run().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exitCode = 1; });
